Report Name: #Site wise Stock Ageing - Item#
Description: This Report will display inventory ageing of all sites based on the inward transactions for respective site at item level. It includes FIFO stock value also. The stock value will be perfect if COGS has run properly. This Ageing will be shown based on yesterday's closing stock. Note : Performance will impact for large data volume
